Drum VRM — third-party notices

Application source: MIT; https://github.com/postmelee/drum-vrm

Generated from package-lock.json, installed npm package license files, Cargo.lock, and locked offline cargo metadata.
The npm inventory is the production dependency closure (declaration-only dependencies are labeled).
The Rust inventory conservatively includes build/proc-macro dependencies; inclusion does not mean every crate is linked into the final WASM.
Build/test-only npm dependencies are separately pinned in package-lock.json and are not shipped as application runtime code.

Mediabunny 1.62.1 is used without modifying its covered source files. Its MPL-2.0 license remains in force.
Exact corresponding source, including src/ and the original LICENSE, is available without charge from:
https://registry.npmjs.org/mediabunny/-/mediabunny-1.62.1.tgz
The package integrity below identifies the published source archive used for this build.
This application's separate source files are MIT licensed; no relicensing of third-party components is implied.

External drum models and embedded textures: Innerscene Five-piece drum kit (CC0-1.0), Full Drum Kit by Batoski (CC-BY-3.0), and Drum Kit by smoj (CC-BY-3.0); see asset notices below. No external drum samples, soundfonts, character models, motion datasets, or ML weights are included. System fonts are selected from the device; no font files are redistributed.
Five-piece drum kit — Innerscene (original model)
License: CC0-1.0
Source: https://www.innerscene.com/tools/library/3d-parts/five-piece-drum-kit-daff10f4
Download: https://www.innerscene.com/api/library/five-piece-drum-kit-daff10f4/download
License: https://creativecommons.org/publicdomain/zero/1.0/
Retrieved: 2026-10-24
Asset SHA-256: 3cf8d568b604e99ce63ee82829d7f25044d996ed6cd42c39bc7f226919d1a5a7
Bundled binary is unmodified.
Application modifications/adaptation: Reflect Z into application coordinates, reverse triangle winding, group by instrument and response role, merge by material, resample maps to maximum 512 pixels, and align actual surface contacts to PerformancePlan targets with uniform per-instrument scaling. Source throne omitted. Tagged shells/cymbals respond while hardware and pedals stay fixed. Floor-connected stand hardware is vertically fitted about the source contact to reach the floor; drum shells, surfaces, pedals and elevated rack mounts keep their proportions.
Full license text: models/innerscene-LICENSE.txt
Exact provenance: models/innerscene-provenance.json

Full Drum Kit — Batoski (original model)
License: CC-BY-3.0
Source: https://poly.pizza/m/1HvDgRF7fX
Download: https://static.poly.pizza/75833e82-6e6b-4ca1-9caa-82b3d9c6b73e.glb
License: https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/3.0/
Retrieved: 2026-10-24
Asset SHA-256: 1ced36a850608f146407ad9387208209c59dd4784377a41c53727632bf00f895
Bundled binary is unmodified.
Application modifications/adaptation: Reflect Z, uniformly scale source units by 0.42, ground by 0.004 units, reverse triangle winding, and separate instrument groups by source names. Separate connected kick spurs and floor-tom legs as fixed hardware. Merge by instrument, response role and material. Omit the stool and second crash because the current engine has one crash role. The source has one rack tom and two floor toms; they fill high/mid/low tom roles. Add original application pedal footboards and bases for kick and hi-hat because the source does not contain independent footboards. Fit actual surface contacts to PerformancePlan targets with uniform per-instrument scaling; tagged shells/cymbals respond while hardware stays fixed. Floor-connected stand hardware is vertically fitted about the source contact to reach the floor; drum shells, surfaces, pedals and elevated rack mounts keep their proportions.
Full license text: models/CC-BY-3.0.txt
Exact provenance: models/batoski-provenance.json

Drum Kit — smoj (original model)
License: CC-BY-3.0
Source: https://poly.pizza/m/7FCPjy-3xmz
Download: https://static.poly.pizza/56bea38d-cb46-43e2-976b-2ed5d0827ffe.glb
License: https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/3.0/
Retrieved: 2026-10-24
Asset SHA-256: 4893bf0cd89542862298456be893bce24742947d31c8293230b817e91629d5de
Bundled binary is unmodified.
Application modifications/adaptation: Reflect X, uniformly scale source units by 1.4, translate Y by 0.504, reverse triangle winding, and separate instruments by an explicit source-node and material inventory. Merge by instrument, response role and material; retain original flat colors and translucent drum surfaces. Omit the stool and carpet. Fit actual surface/footboard contacts to PerformancePlan targets with uniform per-instrument scaling; tagged shells/cymbals respond while hardware and pedals stay fixed. Floor-connected stand hardware is vertically fitted about the source contact to reach the floor; drum shells, surfaces, pedals and elevated rack mounts keep their proportions.
Full license text: models/CC-BY-3.0.txt
Exact provenance: models/smoj-provenance.json

Full Drum Kit — Batoski

Source: https://poly.pizza/m/1HvDgRF7fX
Original download: https://static.poly.pizza/75833e82-6e6b-4ca1-9caa-82b3d9c6b73e.glb
License evidence: https://poly.pizza/m/1HvDgRF7fX
Retrieved: 2026-10-24
Binary SHA-256: 1ced36a850608f146407ad9387208209c59dd4784377a41c53727632bf00f895
Bundled binary unmodified: true
Application modifications/adaptation: Reflect Z, uniformly scale source units by 0.42, ground by 0.004 units, reverse triangle winding, and separate instrument groups by source names. Separate connected kick spurs and floor-tom legs as fixed hardware. Merge by instrument, response role and material. Omit the stool and second crash because the current engine has one crash role. The source has one rack tom and two floor toms; they fill high/mid/low tom roles. Add original application pedal footboards and bases for kick and hi-hat because the source does not contain independent footboards. Fit actual surface contacts to PerformancePlan targets with uniform per-instrument scaling; tagged shells/cymbals respond while hardware stays fixed. Floor-connected stand hardware is vertically fitted about the source contact to reach the floor; drum shells, surfaces, pedals and elevated rack mounts keep their proportions.

Bundled binary unmodified: true
Application modifications/adaptation: Reflect X, uniformly scale source units by 1.4, translate Y by 0.504, reverse triangle winding, and separate instruments by an explicit source-node and material inventory. Merge by instrument, response role and material; retain original flat colors and translucent drum surfaces. Omit the stool and carpet. Fit actual surface/footboard contacts to PerformancePlan targets with uniform per-instrument scaling; tagged shells/cymbals respond while hardware and pedals stay fixed. Floor-connected stand hardware is vertically fitted about the source contact to reach the floor; drum shells, surfaces, pedals and elevated rack mounts keep their proportions.
